# Inventory — pipeline part (§4 generation pipeline, §10 simulation and content, PART II stage 2, PART III village readiness)

> PROGRESS (coherence/inventory-pipeline): COMPLETE 2026-09-29 — all four parts read in full; 806 items, 89 terms, 13 internal conflicts, 3 broken references, 12 wiring gaps, 8 edge gaps; JSON written; result saved to results/coherence_inventory-pipeline.json.

**What this is.** The inventory of the pipeline part of `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` for the coherence, cohesion, wiring and elegance pass. Every normative item of the part — rules, mechanisms, algorithm steps, interfaces and types, numbers, tables, tests and bars, decisions — is listed once, in our own words with its exact values, with every place the unit states it, the unit, base part or volume that owns its subject, and its **wiring**: what it reads or is produced by, and what reads, uses, renders, exports, tests or calls it. Defined terms are listed with every name the text uses for them. The machine-readable copy is `inventory-pipeline.json` (items `PIPELINE-0001` … `PIPELINE-0806`, terms `PIPELINE-T001` … `PIPELINE-T089`, conflicts `CF-PIPE-*`, broken references `BR-PIPE-*`, wiring gaps `WG-PIPE-*`, edge gaps `EDGE-PIPE-*`); its statements are complete, this report truncates long ones.

**Coverage.** Every line of the unit was read in full, in order: §4 (l. 3680–6793), §10 (l. 11435–11888), PART II (l. 15186–16188) and PART III (l. 16189–16267) of `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` md5 `01fea21f…` (17 297 lines) — 4 650 lines, 539 KB, ≈ 85 900 words, 23 % of the file's bytes. No other top-level heading falls to this part (the unclaimed ones go to the scope part). Line numbers below refer to that file.

**How ownership is recorded.** `unit` — the pipeline part is the canonical home. `unit (hook; other end in volume)` — the base carries the hook (record, row, interface, invariant or test) and the named volume builds the other end (215 items; the staged layers of II.6–II.7, the port rows, the evolution engine, the village). `unit (shared with …)` — the pipeline states the stage-side rule and another base part holds the record or mechanism it uses. `base:<part>` — another base part owns the subject; this unit should cite it. `volume` — a volume owns the subject (PART II's close-up interface is V01's at build depth, PART III's is V07's). Row values marked `(rows V11)` are content tables whose rows V11 (content) authors; the base owns the mechanism that reads them.

**The overloaded names, in one list:** "stage" (pipeline stage S0–S15; growth stage k; Works stage; product stage 2 and 3; stage-2 feature S1–S6); "rung" (size-ladder rung; wealth-ladder rung 1–3; elaboration rung outline/wings/rooms/plan; failure-ladder rung; cut-ladder rung); "ladder" (size ladder; wealth ladder; interior ladder; decline ladders; F-* ladders; F-UNDER; the requested-mode basement ladder); "band" (prosperity band; area band; block-depth band; wall half-band; margin band; underground depth band; tree band; riverBandM; dwell band; river class band); "neighbourhood" (a district opened at close-up LOD; an underground neighbourhood; an undercity neighbourhood; item 72's neighbourhood = district); "cluster" (trade-group cluster tag; informal cluster; clustering term; a connected cluster of chambers); "exposure" (Plot.exposure; the decline exposure field E(x); wind exposure; the exposure relation row); "fill" (farm mode; FarmTable.extent value; the generation fill of households; the `fill` hand edit); "calendar" and "clock" (the map's week clock and CalendarRow; the module's hour calendar and time key — CF-PIPE-04); "network" (the street network strategy; the sewer network stage); "condition" (Building.condition; a tunnel's condition reading); "floor" (geometry floor; band range floor; a storey floor; the chamber floor); "field" (prosperity/density/greenery fields; the travel-cost field T; a farm field). Retired synonym named by the text: "character" for the per-district input (now `DistrictIntent`, §7.7; J-A72.41); II.1's `DistrictContext` still lists "character" among its contents without saying it is the CharacterVector.

## 11. Narration, deferral wording, markers

- **Narration to cut** (the validation records keep it):
  - §4.11 l.6322 — '(J-Fpipe-2, from Fable's review of 27 September 2026)' in the carve far-part heading
  - II.2 l.15312 — 'an inference that Fable's evidence gate released as such (item 58 (f))'
  - II.6 (6) l.15500-15501 — 'the program's proposed response, adopted as conversion kinds and vetoable'
  - II.7 l.15587 — 'items 12 and 14 of the owner's amendments of 2026-09-25, evening'
  - §4.11 l.6555-6556 — 'this replaces the rule that an epoch placed delta demand only on new or transformed plots (J-A8.44)'
  - §4.4 l.4518 — 'The alternatives rejected for this construction are recorded with J-P4 and J-3' (pointer, keep short)
  - §4.1 l.3824; §4.11 l.6018; II.7 l.15575; §4.9 l.5818 — owner-dated rulings '(owner, 2026-09-26 …)' — provenance, keep as owner-rule citations
  - §4.11 l.6116-6117 — '(the former "10 % of a district's plots per decade" is the fabric row)'
  - §4.6 step 9 l.5019 — 'the former free-end term is withdrawn — it was a drawing artefact'
  - §4.6 step 4 l.4936-4937 — '(J-A37.41, which supersedes J-P16's perpendicular-ray far side and its fallback chain)'
- **Deferral scan.** No "TBD", "to be decided" or open question in the unit. Every "later" is temporal (a later stage, a later epoch) or names a roadmap-staged subject that keeps its hook: "the owner's later stage (DEF-8)" (§4.11 l.6347), "the engine's step later" (l.6423), "the engine's kinds later" (l.6629), "setting packs later" (D-6, l.11868) — staged by the owner's roadmap, not an unresolved choice.
- **Markers.** The unit carries no per-section review marker (principle (8)); PLAUSIBLE and CONFIRMED labels on numbers are evidence labels and stay.
- **Receipts cited by file** (keep as short pointers): `fixall_pipeline_r2_levelset.out` (0091, 0112), `fixall_pipeline_mfcg_fits.out` (0096, 0191), `fixall_pipeline_r2_quarters.out` (0191), `fixall_pipeline_ou_heading.out` (0165).
